<div class="hs_cos_wrapper hs_cos_wrapper_widget hs_cos_wrapper_type_module" data-hs-cos-general-type="widget" data-hs-cos-type="module" id="hs_cos_wrapper_widget_1560432513543" style=""><div class="cm-two-col-with-img-align v2 right">

<h2>Plan your client’s content the way you prefer</h2>
<p>Schedule beautiful posts individually, through queues or in bulk, and use our interactive calendar to iron out any kinks. <br/><br/>Tailor posts to each social network to maximise organic reach, and optimise images with our in-built editor or design new ones with Canva.</p>

<p>Spot trends through our in-depth reports on most engaging content, optimal posting times per network, and website traffic from social.</p>
<p>Automatically send reports to key stakeholders, team members and clients on a daily, weekly or monthly basis. Or, give access to our live reports to provide more transparency.</p>

<h2><span style="color: #ffffff; font-size: 30px;">As an agency, you’re already pulled in a million directions</span></h2>

<article class="testimonial">
<blockquote>
       My agency uses Sendible to manage all our clients social media accounts, and it has been such an upgrade after a terrible and frustrating experience with Sprout Social. Posting and monitoring accounts is easier and more flexible, plus we don't have any of the technical issues we used to have.
     </blockquote>
